Task: Scaffold inspection, full Prisma schema, core backend libs, seed, auth, app shell

Work Log:
- Reviewed full spec (upload/Pasted Content_1789664979348.txt): enterprise facility maintenance app, green branding, Poppins, floating nav, customer portal, RBAC, workflows.
- Installed `qrcode` + `@types/qrcode`.
- Wrote complete `prisma/schema.prisma`: Users/Roles/Sessions/PasswordReset/TechnicianProfile, Customers, Departments/Employees/Attendance/LeaveRequest, Locations/Equipment (QR token), Complaints+StatusHistory, WorkOrders+Checklist+Materials, PmPlan/PmTask/PmTaskChecklistItem, IrmsProject/InspectionReport/InspectionFinding, Supplier/InventoryItem/StockMovement, PurchaseOrder/PurchaseItem, Quotation/QuotationItem, Invoice/InvoiceItem/Payment, Account/Transaction/Expense, Vehicle, Notification, Document, Setting, AuditLog, Counter, Draft. Money stored as integer cents. `bun run db:push` OK.
- Core libs created:
  - `src/lib/hms/constants.ts` — roles, permission matrix per role (SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN/SUPERVISOR/TECHNICIAN/CUSTOMER/FINANCE/HR), workflow transitions (complaint/WO), status tones, humanize.
  - `src/lib/hms/auth.ts` — scrypt password hashing, DB sessions, HttpOnly SameSite cookie, sliding silent renewal, password policy.

  - `src/lib/hms/rate-limit.ts` — in-memory sliding window (login 8/5min per IP+email).
  - `src/lib/hms/services.ts` — `audit()`, `nextNumber(prefix)` (CPT-2025-0001 style), `notify()`/`notifyRole()` centralized notification service (IN_APP persisted; EMAIL/WHATSAPP/PUSH logged delivery).
  - `src/lib/hms/format.ts` (client-safe money/date), `src/lib/hms/api-client.ts` (fetch wrapper + ClientApiError), `src/lib/hms/ui-store.ts` (zustand: activeModule, deepLink for QR).
- Seeded DB (`bun prisma/seed.ts`): 3 staff + 3 technicians + 1 finance + 1 HR + 3 customer portal users; 3 customers, 4 locations, 8 equipment, 6 complaints across workflow states + status history, 4 work orders (checklists+materials), 5 PM plans + 5 tasks, 3 suppliers, 10 inventory items + opening movements, 1 PO, quotation, 2 invoices + payments, accounts/transactions/expense, 4 employees + attendance 7d + 2 leave requests, 2 IRMS projects + 2 inspection reports + findings, 3 vehicles, settings, notifications, audit logs. All passwords `Password@123`.
- APIs: `/api/v1/auth/{login,logout,session,forgot-password,reset-password,password}`, `/api/v1/dashboard` (role-based, real aggregates), `/api/v1/notifications` (GET/PATCH mark read), `/api/v1/drafts` (server draft backup), `/api/health`, `/api/health/ready` (DB check).
- Frontend shell: green theme + Poppins (`layout.tsx`, `globals.css`), `page.tsx` → SessionProvider → Gate (login screen or AppShell). AppShell: sticky header, desktop floating glass nav, mobile bottom nav + More sheet, notification bell w/ unread badge + mark-all-read, user menu (change password/about/logout), sticky footer w/ health badge, QR deep-link handling (`/?resource=equipment:{qrToken}`).
- Shared UI: `ui-bits.tsx` (StatCard/StatusBadge/PageHeader/Loading/Empty/Error states), `data-table.tsx` (search/filter/sort/pagination/CSV export), `use-draft.ts` hook (debounced localStorage+server draft autosave, restore with consent, beforeunload guard), registry `registry.tsx` (20 modules w/ permission gating).
- Dashboard module fully implemented (role-based KPIs, charts via recharts, recent lists, technician workload, equipment status).
- Smoke-tested via curl: login 200, session cookie works, dashboard returns live aggregates, notifications 2 unread, health/ready DB ok.

Stage Summary:
- DB schema is FROZEN for module agents: do NOT edit prisma/schema.prisma.
- Shared infra is FROZEN: do NOT edit files in src/lib/hms/*, src/hooks/use-draft.ts, src/components/hms/shared/*, shell.tsx, session.tsx, gate.tsx, login-screen.tsx, registry.tsx, page.tsx, layout.tsx, globals.css.

- Login accounts (Password@123): admin@mohdhms.com (SUPER_ADMIN), operations@mohdhms.com (ADMIN), supervisor@mohdhms.com (SUPERVISOR), ahmad.tech|mohdhms.com etc (TECHNICIAN), finance@mohdhms.com (FINANCE), hr@mohdhms.com (HR), customer1@demo.my (CUSTOMER).
- Pattern for every module API: export GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE = handler(async ({req,user}) => …, { permission: PERMISSIONS.x }) with zod parseBody, listQuery+pagedMeta for lists, nextNumber() for codes, audit() on mutations, notify() on workflow events, customer scoping via isStaff/user.customerId.
- Pattern for module UI: replace `src/components/hms/modules/<key>/index.tsx`, export `<X>Module`, use api client + DataTable + ui-bits + useDraft + toast; loading/error/empty states mandatory; responsive; role-gated buttons via `useSession().user.permissions`.

Task ID: 6-d
Agent: module-agent-inventory-purchases
Task: Built Inventory + Purchases modules end-to-end (API routes, UI modules, E2E verified).

Work Log:
- Read worklog + foundation libs (api.ts, services.ts, constants.ts, rbac.ts, format.ts, api-client.ts, data-table.tsx, ui-bits.tsx, use-draft.ts, session.tsx) and prisma schema for Supplier/InventoryItem/StockMovement/PurchaseOrder/PurchaseItem.
- APIs created (9 route files, all handler()-wrapped with permissions, zod parseBody, audit(), notify/notifyRole, integer-cents money, local `withId` helper for Next 16 async params):
  - inventory/route.ts: GET list (search sku/name, category, status, lowStock=1 via `stockQty lte db.inventoryItem.fields.minStockQty`, pagination; per-item computed `low`; meta.stats={total,lowStock,valueCents} for StatCards; supplier {id,name} include — Quotations picker contract) · POST create (sku auto nextNumber("ITM"), 409 on duplicate SKU, opening stock creates RECEIVE movement, audit ITEM_CREATED).
  - inventory/[id]/route.ts: GET detail + last 20 movements · PATCH (sku unique-check, name/category/unit/minStockQty/unitCost/supplierId/status) · DELETE soft-archives to INACTIVE when referenced by stockMovements/purchaseItems, else hard delete.
  - inventory/[id]/movement/route.ts: POST RECEIVE/ISSUE/RETURN/ADJUST (ADJUST = signed delta; non-ADJUST must be positive), $transaction {update stockQty + create movement(balanceAfter)}, ISSUE/ADJUST below 0 → 400 "Insufficient stock", audit STOCK_ADJUSTED, notifyRole ADMIN when new balance <= minStockQty ("Low stock: {sku} {name} at {balance}").
  - inventory/movements/route.ts: GET ledger desc w/ item{sku,name,unit}, filters itemId/type, pagination.
  - suppliers/route.ts + suppliers/[id]/route.ts: GET list w/ search + _count{items,purchaseOrders} · POST (code nextNumber("SUP")) · GET/PATCH/DELETE (soft INACTIVE when referenced by items/POs/expenses, else delete). PATCH schema fields all optional (partial update).
  - purchases/route.ts: GET list incl supplier + _count.items, filters status/supplierId/search(code|supplier.name), meta.stats={awaitingApproval,approvedOpen,receivedThisMonth} · POST (supplier must exist+ACTIVE, itemId existence check, totals server-side: subtotal=Σ round(qty×unitCostCents), tax=round(subtotal×0.06), total; code nextNumber("PO"), status DRAFT, audit PO_CREATED).
  - purchases/[id]/route.ts: GET detail (supplier + items incl item{sku,name,unit}) · PATCH DRAFT-only (notes/expectedDate/items replace via deleteMany+createMany + totals recompute; else 422 INVALID_TRANSITION).
  - purchases/[id]/transition/route.ts: submit (DRAFT→PENDING_APPROVAL, notifyRole FINANCE+ADMIN "PO {code} awaiting approval") · approve/reject (purchases_approve enforced inside handler via roleCan; approve sets approvedById/approvedAt + notifies SUPERVISOR) · receive (APPROVED|PARTIALLY_RECEIVED; body items[{purchaseItemId,quantity}] optional, default = all remaining; guards: line ownership 400, over-receive 400; $transaction updates receivedQty, increments InventoryItem.stockQty and writes RECEIVE movement referenceType=PURCHASE only for lines with itemId; all received → RECEIVED+receivedAt else PARTIALLY_RECEIVED) · cancel (DRAFT|PENDING_APPROVAL|APPROVED→CANCELLED).
- UI: replaced stubs with full InventoryModule (StatCards from server meta.stats; Tabs Items/Movements/Suppliers; items DataTable w/ red low-stock highlight + Low-stock toolbar filter + CSV "inventory"; Add-item dialog with useDraft("inventory.item.create") incl restore/discard banner; Edit + Adjust-stock (type/qty/note) + delete-confirm dialogs; movements table w/ signed qty +green/−red + type badge; suppliers table + create/edit/delete) and PurchasesModule (StatCards; PO table w/ row-click detail; New-PO dialog: supplier select, expected date, dynamic lines w/ inventory picker OR free text + qty + unit-cost RM + line total + subtotal/tax 6%/grand preview, useDraft("purchase.create"); detail dialog: items table w/ receivedQty, totals, role/status-gated Submit/Approve/Reject/Receive(per-line qty defaulting to remaining)/Cancel).
- Verified: `bunx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors in my 11 files (pre-existing errors remain in other agents' files + foundation src/lib/hms/constants.ts); eslint clean on my files; curl E2E (admin): item create/opening movement/dup-SKU 409, ISSUE/ADJUST/RETURN balances, insufficient-stock 400, ADJUST 0 validation, lowStock filter, movements filters, supplier CRUD + soft/hard delete, PO create→totals math verified (3×12.50+2×8.25=5400c+324c tax=5724c; draft-edit recompute 7900c/474c/8374c)→submit→approve→partial receive→RECEIVED with stock increments + PURCHASE movements, over-receive/double-receive/approve-while-draft 400/422 guards, supervisor 403 on item create, finance user received "PO … awaiting approval" notification, admin received low-stock warning; dev.log shows zero error-level entries for my routes (non-2xx are intentional guard tests).
- Cleanup: removed throwaway probe records; kept one completed PO trail (PO-2026-0001/0002) + archived test items/supplier as audit evidence. Verify scripts left in /home/z/tmp-verify/.

Stage Summary:
- Endpoints: GET/POST /api/v1/inventory · GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/v1/inventory/[id] · POST /api/v1/inventory/[id]/movement · GET /api/v1/inventory/movements · GET/POST /api/v1/suppliers · GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/v1/suppliers/[id] · GET/POST /api/v1/purchases · GET/PATCH /api/v1/purchases/[id] · POST /api/v1/purchases/[id]/transition {action: submit|approve|reject|receive|cancel}.
- Contracts kept: GET /api/v1/inventory returns okList items {id, sku, name, category, unit, stockQty, minStockQty, unitCostCents, supplierId, supplier:{id,name}|null, status, low} + ?lowStock=1 + search + pagination (Quotations agent can rely on this). All money integer cents, tax = round(subtotal×0.06) server-side only. Stock never negative; every stock change writes a StockMovement with balanceAfter.
- Notes for other agents / desired shared changes (NOT implemented, shared files are frozen):
  1. src/lib/hms/constants.ts STATUS_TONE has a duplicate `OVERDUE` key (lines ~207/219) — tsc flags it in foundation file; second value wins, harmless at runtime but should be deduped by foundation owner.
  2. data-table.tsx is client-side-only (search/sort/pagination over a loaded page); for large datasets a server-driven mode (optional props) would help future modules.
  3. A shared `useApiList` hook (fetch/loading/error/meta) would remove per-module boilerplate; each module re-implements it today.
  4. Transient: another agent's src/components/hms/modules/pm/index.tsx briefly broke the shared dev compile ("completeTask defined multiple times") causing 500 HTML on all routes — keep modules compiling before handing off.

- Created 7 API route files (all zod-validated, handler() wrapped, audited):
  - /api/v1/reports GET (reports_read): type=complaints|work_orders|equipment|pm_compliance|finance|technicians, from/to YYYY-MM-DD (default last 30 days, validated), ok({type,from,to,summary,rows[,expenses]}). Complaints/workOrders filtered by createdAt, PM tasks by dueDate, finance invoices by invoiceDate + expenses by expenseDate (booked summary figures exclude DRAFT/CANCELLED, consistent with dashboard), equipment is point-in-time, technicians report counts openWOs/completedWOs(in range)/openComplaints per tech. Defensive customer scoping via isStaff.
  - /api/v1/reports/export GET (reports_export): reuses the reports pipeline (imports GET from ../route), streams BOM-prefixed CSV, Content-Disposition attachment; filename report-{type}-{from}-to-{to}.csv; forwards inner errors untouched.

Audits VEHICLE_UPDATED with changed-field list.

Company = form built from GET keys (15 seeded keys incl. emails, tax_percent_default, currency, invoice/quotation term textareas), disabled unless settings_manage, Save PUTs only dirty values + success toast. System (admin-only module) = App name/version card, GET /api/health + GET /api/health/ready rows with real "Run check" fetches showing ok/fail icon, HTTP status, status/checks + timestamp, DB note "SQLite via Prisma (sandbox) — PostgreSQL in production deployment", session card (role/email/name).

Vehicles: GET list w/ technician include, POST (code VEH-2026-0001, technician claim removed VEH-001's assignee), PATCH status/odometer, dup rego 409, bad tech 400, DELETE → RETIRED, partial PATCH preserves un-sent dates, all 3 audit entries written. Task ID: 6-f
Agent: module-agent-pm-irms-hr
Task: Built PM (plans/tasks/checklist lifecycle), IRMS (projects/reports/submit-approve/print) and HR (overview/attendance/leave/departments) modules — APIs + UI.

Work Log:
- Read worklog + foundation libs (api.ts, constants.ts, services.ts, rbac.ts, api-client.ts, use-draft.ts, session.tsx, DataTable, ui-bits); followed vehicles module conventions for UI.
- PM API: plans GET/POST (nextNumber PM, checklistTemplate JSON.stringify, nextDue default now+FREQUENCY_DAYS, audit PM_PLAN_CREATED), plans/[id] GET+recentTasks/PATCH (items replace, freq change re-baselines next due)/DELETE→active=false soft, plans/[id]/generate POST (pm_manage OR pm_execute via in-handler roleCan; creates PMT task + checklist items from template in $transaction, advances plan.nextDueDate by FREQUENCY_DAYS, audit PM_TASK_GENERATED, notify assigned technician).
- PM tasks: GET auto-rollover updateMany SCHEDULED+past-due→OVERDUE before query; filters status/overdue=1/technicianId/mine=1 (own technicianProfile)/planId/search + pagination. tasks/[id] GET detail+checklist (also freshens overdue state), PATCH (technicianId/dueDate/notes, pm_manage, blocked when COMPLETED/SKIPPED). tasks/[id]/transition POST start|complete|skip: start SCHEDULED/OVERDUE→IN_PROGRESS (assigned tech or pm_execute/manage); complete requires ALL checklist done unless force+pm_manage ($transaction marks items done, completedAt, plan.lastCompletedAt), audit PM_COMPLETED + notifyRole SUPERVISOR; skip pm_manage only → SKIPPED.
- IRMS API: projects GET (customer + inspections count, search/status), POST (nextNumber PRJ, status ACTIVE, date validation, audit IRMS_PROJECT_CREATED); [id] GET (+20 inspections w/ counts)/PATCH/DELETE — delete only when zero reports, else status=COMPLETED (audit IRMS_PROJECT_COMPLETED). reports GET (project/equipment/inspector, filters status/projectId/type/search), POST (nextNumber INS, status DRAFT, inspectorId=own technicianProfile by userId, findings created inline, audit INSPECTION_CREATED); [id] GET detail (project+customer/equipment/inspector/findings), PATCH DRAFT-only ($transaction replaces findings), DELETE DRAFT-only; transition submit (manage or assigned inspector; notifyRole ADMIN w/ EMAIL channel + inspector self-notify) / approve (irms_manage; notify inspector), audit INSPECTION_SUBMITTED/APPROVED.
- HR API: overview GET (headcount/active/onLeaveToday/presentToday/absentToday/halfDayToday/pendingLeaves, departments w/ counts, attendance7d computed from attendance day-ranges, hr_read); departments GET (employees_read, w/ counts) POST (hr_manage, unique name→409 CONFLICT); attendance GET ?date= or ?from&to with employee join, POST hr_manage upsert by (employeeId, day-range match) accepting HH:MM or ISO times, audit ATTENDANCE_MARKED; leave GET hr_read (status/employeeId filters, approver name resolved via users since approvedById has no Prisma relation), POST hr_read — self-filing resolves own employee via employee.userId, employeeId override restricted to SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN/HR, days computed server-side inclusive; leave/[id] PATCH hr_manage approve|reject → status+approvedById/At, audit LEAVE_APPROVED/REJECTED, notify employee.userId if linked.
- UI PmModule: StatCards (active plans, due this week, overdue, completed this month); Tabs Plans/Tasks. Plans: DataTable w/ frequency StatusBadge, red next-due when due, inline active Switch (pm_manage), Generate row action + "Generate due tasks" toolbar (loops plans due <= today). New Plan dialog uses useDraft "pm.plan.create" (name/equipment/frequency/technician/checklist labels one-per-line/next due) with restore + graceful degradation when equipment/technician lists unavailable (403/404). Tasks: due-date red for overdue, Start/Complete/Skip row actions; Complete dialog loads checklist, toggles persist via transition checklist payload, button enabled only when all done, notes field. Technician role auto-loads mine=1.
- UI IrmsModule: Tabs Projects/Reports. Projects: create (useDraft "irms.project.create") + edit dialog (status select), delete → API guard (no reports = delete; has reports = COMPLETED). Reports: create dialog with dynamic findings rows (finding/severity/recommendation) via useDraft "irms.report.create"; detail dialog w/ meta grid, findings table, Submit (DRAFT), Approve (SUBMITTED, irms_manage), Print → print-only document (MOHD.HMS ENTERPRISE header, meta table, findings, summary, signature lines) via component-local @media print scoping (#irms-print-doc) — no globals.css changes.
- UI HrModule: overview KPI cards + recharts stacked 7d attendance bar (Present/Half/Leave/Absent) + departments list w/ counts + add-department dialog; Tabs Employees (read-only DataTable from /api/v1/employees, graceful amber notice + retry when register unavailable — management lives in Employees module), Attendance (date picker, status badges, mark/edit dialog w/ time inputs disabled for ABSENT/LEAVE, hr_manage), Leave (status filter, New request dialog — self vs on-behalf per role, Approve/Reject inline for PENDING, hr_manage).
- Verified: bunx tsc --noEmit clean for ALL owned files (fixed listQuery dir widening via `as const` orderBy + renamed PM completeTask handler). curl E2E with admin cookie: plan create → generate (PMT created w/ checklist, nextDue advanced 30d) → complete-blocked-422 → start → complete w/ checklist (COMPLETED, lastCompletedAt set); IRMS project → report w/ findings → approve-while-draft-422 → submit → patch-422 → approve → delete-guard (project → COMPLETED); HR overview live aggregates, attendance create+upsert(200), leave create(days=5 server-computed) → approve → double-decision-422, dept create + duplicate-409; overdue rollover verified (SCHEDULED past-due → OVERDUE on list GET); RBAC: technician (pm_execute) can generate/start, skip→403, hr/overview→403. Audit actions confirmed in AuditLog (PM_PLAN_CREATED, PM_TASK_GENERATED, PM_TASK_STARTED, PM_COMPLETED, IRMS_PROJECT_CREATED/COMPLETED, INSPECTION_CREATED/SUBMITTED/APPROVED, ATTENDANCE_MARKED, LEAVE_REQUESTED/APPROVED, DEPARTMENT_CREATED) + notifications for technician. dev.log: zero errors for my routes. Cleaned E2E test records from dev DB afterwards (counters keep their sequence).

Stage Summary:

Task ID: 6-e
Agent: module-agent-quotations-invoices-finance
Task: Built Quotations, Invoices and Finance modules end-to-end (API + UI) with server-side document math, payment workflow, expense approval and ledger integration.

Work Log:
- Read worklog + foundation libs (api.ts, constants.ts, services.ts, rbac.ts, format.ts, api-client.ts, session.tsx, ui-bits, data-table, use-draft, use-toast) and prisma schema for Quotation/Invoice/Payment/Account/Transaction/Expense/WorkOrder.
- API quotations: list (scope+filters+pagination, customer incl.), create (zod, server math, nextNumber QTN, audit QUOTATION_CREATED), [id] GET/PATCH(draft only, full item replace via deleteMany+createMany)/DELETE(draft only), transition send|approve|reject|expire (notify customer portal user on send + notifyRole FINANCE), convert APPROVED→CONVERTED in $transaction (creates draft invoice, copies totals/items recomputed identically, sets convertedInvoiceId, dueDate +30d).
- API invoices: list runs overdue sweep (updateMany SENT+dueDate<now → OVERDUE) BEFORE query and returns meta {overdueCount, outstandingCents, paidThisMonthCents}; create manual OR {workOrderId} (validates COMPLETED + not already invoiced, builds LABOUR line "Labour — {wo.title}" qty=labourHours unit=hr tax 6 and MATERIAL lines from wo.materials tax 6, links WorkOrder.invoiceId in the same $transaction → double-invoice guard 409); [id] GET detail incl. items+payments+customer+quotation/workOrder refs, PATCH/DELETE draft-only; transition send(sentAt=now, notify portal user "Invoice {code}" + FINANCE)|cancel(DRAFT|SENT|OVERDUE); [id]/payments POST (payments_record): validates amount>0 ≤ balance, $transaction {create Payment(nextNumber PAY, customerId, recordedById) → invoice.paidCents/balanceCents/status PAID|PARTIALLY_PAID → ACC-BANK increment → INCOME SERVICE_INCOME transaction (nextNumber TRX, referenceType PAYMENT, description "Payment {code}")}, audit PAYMENT_RECORDED + notifyRole FINANCE, returns updated invoice.
- API finance: summary (kpis income/expenses/netProfit/receivables/invoicedTotal/collectedTotal/pendingApprovalExpenses; 6-month income vs expense computed in JS from fetched transactions; accounts; recentTransactions take 10 incl. account; receivables take 20 balanceCents>0 incl. customer; recentExpenses take 8); expenses list/create (EXP, PENDING)/[id] GET+PATCH while PENDING/transition approve(PENDING→APPROVED, approvedById)|mark_paid(APPROVED→PAID, EXPENSE transaction on ACC-CASH-first/bank-fallback + balance decrement); transactions list (type + month YYYY-MM filters, incl. account).
- UI QuotationsModule: stat cards (draft/sent/approved counts + converted value), DataTable (code/customer/date/valid until/total/status, status filter, CSV), New Quotation dialog (useDraft "quotation.create", customer select from /api/v1/customers, inventory picker from /api/v1/inventory prefills description/unit/price, dynamic rows, live totals footer mirroring server math exactly, notes/terms), detail dialog (document preview with company block from /api/v1/settings with "MOHD.HMS Enterprise" fallback, Send/Approve/Reject/Expire/Convert/Delete by status+perm, Print), print-only container (hidden print:block; screen content print:hidden).
- UI InvoicesModule: stat cards from list meta (outstanding, overdue count, collected this month), New Invoice dialog with source toggle (manual items / from completed WO — WO option auto-hides when /api/v1/work-orders 403s or returns none), detail dialog (invoice doc preview with Paid/Balance, payments table, quotation/WO refs, Send/Cancel/Delete/Print by status+perm), Record Payment dialog (amount RM, method select, reference, note, live new-balance/status preview).
- UI FinanceModule: finance_read guard (friendly empty state → Invoices for CUSTOMER), Tabs Overview/Receivables/Expenses/Transactions: KPI StatCards, recharts BarChart income(green)/expense(amber) 6 months, accounts cards, recent transactions list; Receivables table (row click → invoices module); Expenses table + Add expense dialog (useDraft "finance.expense.create") + Approve/Mark-paid row actions; Transactions table + month input + type filter + API-backed month param. TypeBadge for Income/Expense.
- Fixes during verify: InvoiceItem has NO itemId column (unlike QuotationItem) → strip before persisting; Prisma update types → typed UncheckedUpdateInput + createMany; work order double-invoicing → invoiceId link inside $transaction.
- curl-verified full chain (see Stage Summary for figures); customer jar scoping verified; tsc clean for owned files; dev.log clean for owned routes after fix.

Stage Summary:
- Endpoints: /api/v1/quotations (GET,POST), /quotations/[id] (GET,PATCH,DELETE), /quotations/[id]/transition (POST send|approve|reject|expire), /quotations/[id]/convert (POST); /api/v1/invoices (GET+overdue sweep+meta, POST manual|workOrderId), /invoices/[id] (GET,PATCH,DELETE), /invoices/[id]/transition (POST send|cancel), /invoices/[id]/payments (POST payments_record); /api/v1/finance/summary (GET), /finance/expenses (GET,POST), /finance/expenses/[id] (GET,PATCH while PENDING), /finance/expenses/[id]/transition (POST approve|mark_paid), /finance/transactions (GET type/month filters).
- Math (server = UI mirror): unitPriceCents=round(price×100); itemTotal=round(qty×unitPriceCents×(1−disc%/100)); subtotal=ΣitemTotals; taxCents=Σround(itemTotal×tax%/100); discount/shipping=round(input×100); total=max(0, subtotal−discount+tax+shipping); labour/materialCost=Σ LABOUR/MATERIAL itemTotals. Verified to the cent: qty3×RM1250.50−10%+6% → 337635c, tax 20258c; total 381493c.
- Payment flow: balance=total−paid; status balance==0→PAID, paid>0→PARTIALLY_PAID; credits ACC-BANK, writes INCOME SERVICE_INCOME "Payment {inv.code}" TRX. mark_paid writes EXPENSE TRX on ACC-CASH (bank fallback) and debits account. Expense PATCH only while PENDING; over-payment rejected 400; WO double-invoice 409; invalid transitions 422; customers see only own quotations/invoices (404 on others' detail, 403 on manage).
- Contracts used: GET /api/v1/customers, /api/v1/inventory, /api/v1/work-orders?status=COMPLETED (403 handled by hiding WO option), /api/v1/settings (company_name with fallback) — all optional/guarded.
- NOTE for orchestrator: pre-existing tsc errors outside my files: src/lib/hms/constants.ts duplicate OVERDUE key in STATUS_TONE (TS1117); src/lib/hms/auth.ts ResponseCookies .catch; seed.ts type errors; several module routes by other agents (hr/irms/pm/technicians/work-orders _lib imports). Not touched per ownership rules.

Includes: COMPLAINT_INCLUDE / COMPLAINT_DETAIL_INCLUDE, WO_INCLUDE / WO_DETAIL_INCLUDE (Prisma `satisfies` typing so payload types resolve), scope/visibility assertions, technician-profile lookup, WO totals recalc, transition guard.
- Complaints API: GET list (status/priority/customerId/technicianId/mine=1/search over code+title+description, pagination via listQuery+pagedMeta, role scoping: CUSTOMER→own customerId, TECHNICIAN→assigned-to-profile OR created-by-self, staff→all) and POST create (customer role forces own customerId; staff must pass customerId; validates customer + equipment-ownership; code=nextNumber("CPT"); status NEW + initial statusHistory row; audit COMPLAINT_CREATED; notifyRole SUPERVISOR+ADMIN).
- Complaints [id]: GET detail (statusHistory with resolved changedBy names — schema stores changedById without a relation, so names resolved via explicit user query; workOrders list; 404 if missing, 403 if out of scope) + PATCH (only while NEW, creator or complaints_update, equipment ownership validated, audited).
- Complaints [id]/transition POST {action,technicianId?,note?}: assign (complaints_assign; NEW→ASSIGNED; validates TechnicianProfile; notify technician + customer portal user), accept/start (only assigned technician; ASSIGNED→IN_PROGRESS; start is alias + idempotent no-op when already IN_PROGRESS), complete (assigned technician or complaints_update; resolutionNotes from note), confirm (customer portal owner OR complaints_update; customerFeedback from note; notifies ADMIN+FINANCE "ready for invoicing"), close (complaints_close), cancel (complaints_assign; from NEW/ASSIGNED/IN_PROGRESS). Every transition validated against COMPLAINT_TRANSITIONS map → Errors.invalidTransition("<from> → <to> is not allowed") (422), history row on each, audit on each, portal user resolved via include customer.portalUser.
- Work Orders API: GET list (status/customerId/technicianId/complaintId/search code+title, role scoping incl. technician→own profile) and POST create (validates customer/equipment-ownership/complaint-belongs-to-customer/technician-profile; materialsTotalCents=Σ round(qty*unitCostCents); labourTotalCents=0; totalCents=materialsTotal; nested checklist create sortOrder=index; code=nextNumber("WO"); status PENDING; audit WORK_ORDER_CREATED; notify assigned technician; returns full detail incl. checklist+materials).
- Work Orders [id]: GET detail (checklist sortOrder asc, materials + inventoryItem, customer/equipment/technician/complaint; scoped); PATCH with per-field permission split: core fields (title/description/priority/scheduledDate/notes) need work_orders_update; technicianId needs work_orders_assign (status must be PENDING/ACCEPTED/ON_HOLD, notifies new technician); labourHours/labourRateCents only while IN_PROGRESS by assigned technician or work_orders_update → labourTotalCents=round(hours*rate), totalCents=labour+materials.
- Work Orders [id]/transition POST {action,note?}: accept/start/hold/resume (assigned technician or work_orders_update), complete (assigned technician or work_orders_complete; $transaction: re-read status guard for idempotency, recompute totals from current rows, deduct stock per inventoried material (inventoryItem.stockQty -= qty + StockMovement ISSUE quantity=-qty balanceAfter referenceType=WORK_ORDER referenceId=wo.id createdById), linked complaint NOT auto-completed), cancel (assigned technician or work_orders_assign, from PENDING/ACCEPTED/ON_HOLD). start cascades linked complaint ASSIGNED→IN_PROGRESS with its own history row. All validated against WO_TRANSITIONS → 422 on violation.
- Work Orders [id]/checklist: POST {label} (assigned tech or work_orders_update; blocked once COMPLETED/CANCELLED; sortOrder=count) + PATCH {itemId,done} (same roles; blocked after COMPLETED; doneAt=now on check, null on uncheck).
- Work Orders [id]/materials: POST {inventoryItemId?,name,quantity,unitCostCents} (validates inventory item exists; row totalCents=round(qty*cost); recomputes materialsTotalCents/totalCents) + DELETE ?materialId= (same recompute); both blocked once COMPLETED/CANCELLED (stock already deducted).
- UI ComplaintsModule (replaced stub): StatCards (total/open/inProgress/resolved/closed/urgentActive from full list client-side), status tab chips with counts, DataTable (code/title/priority+status badges/customer/technician/equipment/created + priority filter + CSV export), New Complaint dialog gated by complaints.create with useDraft("complaint.create") (title/description/priority Select/customer Select staff-only via /api/v1/customers/equipment Select via /api/v1/equipment?customerId=), draft restore banner, detail Dialog with full info + status timeline (badges, who, when, note) + linked work orders + contextual role-gated actions (Assign technician via /api/v1/technicians; Accept&Start for assigned technician; Complete with resolution notes; Confirm with feedback for portal owner or complaints_update; Close for complaints_close; Cancel for staff) → toast + list reload on success.

Deviations / notes:
1. users/edit-page uses the spec-directed list-based lookup (GET /api/v1/users + find by id). For the record, a GET /api/v1/users/{id} endpoint DOES exist in the backend — the documented list approach was kept anyway (keeps behavior explicit; portal users intentionally resolve to the not-found state).

5. Customer create page has no explicit "Save Draft" button — drafts are automatic (as in the original dialog); hint text + restore banner cover the workflow.

- Inventoried every route under src/app/api: grepped all route.ts for exported methods + permission opts; read dynamic [id] routes to confirm the local `withId(permission, fn)` bridge (awaits Next-16 Promise params, delegates to handler). Noted two-tier RBAC on transition routes (outer *read* permission + inner roleCan per action: WO accept/start/hold/resume/complete/cancel; PO approve/reject via purchases_approve; PM execute vs manage; IRMS submit=inspector vs approve=manage).
- Read services.ts (audit/notify/notifyRole/nextNumber), rate-limit.ts (in-memory sliding window; login 8/5min per IP+email), complaints/_lib.ts + work-orders/_lib.ts (scope helpers complaintScopeWhere/assertViewComplaint/assertViewWorkOrder, recalcWorkOrderTotals, assertWoTransition).
- Frontend: api-client.ts (api.get/post/patch/put/del, credentials:"same-origin", ApiEnvelope/ClientApiError), session.tsx (SessionProvider context + useSession + hasPerm, 4-min heartbeat to /api/v1/auth/session), registry.tsx (MODULES with permissions/roles arrays), shell.tsx (MODULES.filter → visible nav; hashchange dirty guard → "Leave with unsaved changes?" Dialog), ui-store.ts (zustand: activeModule/pages/pageDirty/deepLink), router.ts (hash router helpers, SUFFIX_VIEWS), use-draft.ts (useDraft localStorage `hms:draft:{formKey}` + debounced server backup + useDirtyGuard), drafts route (Draft table upsert, 100KB cap, password/token rejected).
- Redis/caching: grepped repo for redis|ioredis|upstash|cache|Cache-Control|revalidate — NO Redis exists (no dependency, no client); only in-memory rate limiter and `Cache-Control: no-store` on CSV export. Recorded as a spec-vs-reality deviation.
- Verified db.ts Prisma singleton and seed.ts demo accounts (admin@/operations@/supervisor@/finance@/hr@mohdhms.com + 3 customer portal users, CUS-000N seed codes vs API's CUS-2025-NNNN).

Stage Summary:
- 42 Prisma models (SQLite); cuid() PKs; business codes via `nextNumber(prefix)` = `PREFIX-YYYY-NNNN` (Counter table), 18 prefixes; money as integer cents; no Prisma enums — statuses are plain strings + constants.ts arrays.
- Auth is DB-session (opaque token, HttpOnly cookie), NOT JWT; SessionUser carries `permissions: Permission[]`.
- RBAC: `handler(fn, { permission })` from `@/lib/hms/api` + `roleCan/can` from `@/lib/hms/rbac`; dynamic routes bridge Next-16 Promise params via local `withId(permission, fn)`; transition routes use outer read-perm + inner per-action gates; catalog = 47 keys in `src/lib/hms/constants.ts` PERMISSIONS + ROLE_PERMISSIONS (SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN=all).
- Client: fetch wrapper `src/lib/hms/api-client.ts` (no auto-401 handling; heartbeat refresh via SessionProvider); client RBAC = React Context `useSession()` + `hasPerm()`; module visibility = registry.tsx permissions/roles filtered in shell.tsx.
- No Redis / no cache invalidation layer (in-memory rate-limit only). Drafts: localStorage + Draft table backup via /api/v1/drafts; dirty guard = ui-store.pageDirty + shell hashchange Dialog + useDraft beforeunload.

Task ID: 24
Agent: Z.ai Code (main orchestrator)
Task: Implement Google sign-in (OAuth 2.0) feature

Work Log:
- Explored existing auth: custom DB-backed sessions (opaque token, HttpOnly `hms_session` cookie, sliding renewal, scrypt passwords), `handler()` RBAC wrapper, login rate limiting. Decision: implement native Google OAuth 2.0 Authorization Code flow + PKCE on top of the existing session primitives — NextAuth was deliberately NOT used (would duplicate/conflict with the DB-session architecture).
- Sign-in policy (enterprise-safe): Google login works for EXISTING accounts only. The Google-verified email is matched to a User (googleId first, then email) and the account is auto-linked on first use. NO open self-registration; disabled accounts rejected; `email_verified` enforced.
- `prisma/schema.prisma`: added `User.googleId String? @unique` + `User.avatarUrl String?`; `bun run db:push` OK (additive, zero data loss).
- New `src/lib/hms/google-auth.ts` (server-only): config detection (empty env = feature disabled), external origin via x-forwarded-host/proto, redirect-URI resolution (GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI override or `<origin>/api/v1/auth/google/callback`), PKCE S256 pair, constant-time state compare, Google auth-URL builder (openid email profile, prompt=select_account, optional login_hint), code→token exchange, userinfo fetch, error-redirect helper. Endpoint overrides (GOOGLE_AUTH/TOKEN/USERINFO_ENDPOINT) supported for testing doubles.
- `GET /api/v1/auth/google` (start): 307 to Google consent; issues single-use httpOnly state+PKCE-verifier cookies (path `/api/v1/auth/google`, 10 min); rate limited 30/5min/IP; `?googleError=not_configured` bounce when unconfigured.
- `GET /api/v1/auth/google/callback`: validates state (timing-safe, single-use), exchanges code with PKCE, fetches verified profile, links/creates session via existing `createSession()` primitive, sets `hms_session` cookie, audits `LOGIN_GOOGLE` / `LOGIN_GOOGLE_FAILED` (with reason + email), lands on `/#/dashboard`. All failure paths → `/?googleError=<stable-code>`; access_denied silent.
- `login-screen.tsx`: official 4-color Google G (inline SVG), "Sign in with Google" outline button under an "or" divider, redirect-busy state, `?googleError=` → friendly message map (incl. dynamic not_configured message printing the exact callback URL to register), URL cleaned via history.replaceState, disabled while form busy.
- `.env`: documented placeholders (GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID/SECRET empty by default + optional overrides).
- QA infrastructure: `mini-services/mock-google-idp/` (bun, port 3060, /auth /token /userinfo, identity via login_hint) used as a testing double; env-overridable endpoints made this possible without touching production code paths.
- QA (curl): start → 307 with client_id/redirect_uri/state/PKCE + 2 cookies; no-cookie callback → state_mismatch; valid state + bogus code → exchange_failed; FULL round-trip (login_hint=admin@mohdhms.com) → session cookie → `/api/v1/auth/session` authenticated as SUPER_ADMIN → final redirect `/#/dashboard`; second login hit googleId branch; stranger@gmail.com → no_account; state replay → state_mismatch (single-use). Audit rows LOGIN_GOOGLE ×2 + LOGIN_GOOGLE_FAILED(no_account) verified. DB showed googleId set + lastLoginAt updated.
- QA (browser): click "Sign in with Google" → full mock flow → dashboard as SUPER_ADMIN (screenshot google-login-dashboard.png); clean env click → clear not-configured alert with callback URL (google-not-configured.png); password login regression OK (password-login-regression.png); console clean (only realtime retries caused by localhost test browser bypassing the caddy gateway — socket.io handshake verified OK via :81; not an app defect).
- Sandbox ops notes: (a) OOM killer killed next-server at 1.7GB RSS (4GB box) — stale headless Chrome from a previous session was the trigger; killed it; (b) `setsid`-launched background processes are reaped between tool calls in this sandbox NOW — the working pattern is double-fork: `(cmd < /dev/null > log 2>&1 &)`. Dev server + realtime-service (port 3003, was down after process kills) both restarted this way.
- Cleanup: mock IdP stopped; admin.googleId reset to null; 4 LOGIN_GOOGLE* audit rows removed; sessions left intact; dev server running clean (no mock env) on :3000.

Stage Summary:
- Google OAuth is production-ready but INERT until real credentials exist: set GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID + GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET (Google Cloud Console → OAuth 2.0 Web client) and register `<app-origin>/api/v1/auth/google/callback` as an Authorized redirect URI. The login screen then reports exactly this URL if misconfigured.
- New endpoints: GET /api/v1/auth/google, GET /api/v1/auth/google/callback (public, rate-limited, audited). New env knobs (all optional): GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID, GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET, GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI, GOOGLE_AUTH_ENDPOINT, GOOGLE_TOKEN_ENDPOINT, GOOGLE_USERINFO_ENDPOINT. New User fields: googleId (@unique), avatarUrl.
- Guarantees: password login/emails/status/dates/PDF flows untouched; no second company/config source; no open registration (existing accounts only, linked by Google-verified email); RBAC unchanged (Google session == password session).
- mini-services/mock-google-idp is a QA double only — never point production at it.

Notifications: `services.ts notify()/notifyRole()` (IN_APP persisted; PUSH channel was a logged stub). Assets: `public/brand/*` official logo/icons (192/512/apple-touch existed; no maskable). No existing SW/manifest/PWA packages. DB: Prisma (committed schema targets postgresql for production; sandbox runs SQLite via pre-rebase client).

IMPLEMENTATION:
- Icons: `scripts/gen-pwa-icons.py` (PIL) generated `icon-maskable-192/512.png` — full-bleed background sampled from the official badge's interior dark green (#0c2414), logo at 66% (inside maskable safe zone) — plus regenerated `apple-touch-icon.png` with solid brand bg. Official logo preserved, never distorted/replaced.
- Manifest: `src/app/manifest.ts` → `/manifest.webmanifest` (name MOHD.HMS ENTERPRISE, short_name MOHD.HMS, standalone, portrait-primary, theme #0c2414, bg white, lang en-BN, dir ltr, start_url "/", scope "/", 4 icons: 2 any + 2 maskable).
- Service worker `public/sw.js` (framework-free, VERSION v2): navigations = network-first (3.5s timeout) → shell cache → `public/offline.html` fallback; static (`/_next/static`, `/brand`, fonts, images, manifest) = fresh-first with cache fallback; **bypassed entirely**: all `/api/*`, any `XTransformPort`/`EIO`/webpack-hmr request, non-GET, cross-origin — APIs/realtime never intercepted, zero sensitive business data cached (§7). Precache: offline.html + icons. Versioned caches + activation cleanup + shell LRU (10). storableResponse(): rewrites `Cache-Control: no-store` on STORED copies (dev-server quirk) without touching live responses.
- CRITICAL SW lesson (debugged): cache.put() scheduled via event.waitUntil() inside respondWith gets discarded once the response settles in Chromium — puts are now awaited BEFORE respondWith resolves (`storeResponse`). Also fixed handleStatic dropping its cached fallback on network failure (offline boot was broken; now the full SPA shell + chunks hydrate offline).
- offline.html: brand-styled self-contained offline page (no external deps) with Retry button + auto-return via `online` event → /dashboard.
- Client runtime `src/lib/hms/pwa.ts` + `src/components/hms/pwa-runtime.tsx` (mounted once in root layout): deferred SW registration (requestIdleCallback; localStorage `hms:pwa:off` kill-switch §31), user-consented update protocol (waiting SW → "New version available" dialog → Refresh now → SKIP_WAITING → single reload; drafts auto-saved locally so unsaved forms are safe §19), beforeinstallprompt capture (dismissal stored 14 days §17), standalone detection (display-mode + iOS navigator.standalone), offline pill (subtle, non-blocking §21), offline→online edge → "Back online — synchronizing…" pill + "Synchronization complete" toast + realtime resync publish + draft flush event.
- Layout metadata: manifest link, appleWebApp (capable/title/statusBarStyle), viewportFit cover, themeColor #0c2414. Header: safe-area-top padding; mobile bottom nav already had safe-area-bottom (§22/24 — no navigation changes).
- Push (§14-16): `web-push` lib; VAPID keys generated → gitignored `.env` (private key server-only §16); Prisma `PushSubscription` model (endpoint @unique, keys, device/platform/browser, revokedAt, @@index([userId, revokedAt])) — committed schema stays postgresql; local push via temp sqlite schema copy; `src/lib/hms/push-server.ts` (per-user delivery, 404/410 auto-revoke, TTL 3600, never blocking); `services.ts notify()` now delivers real Web Push for every in-app notification (RBAC inherent: recipients are exactly the business event's targets); APIs `GET /api/v1/push/status` (enabled/publicKey/per-device subscribed), `POST|DELETE /api/v1/push/subscribe` (zod, rate-limited, audited, upsert by endpoint = device replacement, multi-device per user).
- Push UI `src/components/hms/shell/pwa-menu.tsx`: bell dropdown footer row (real state only §41: hidden when unsupported/unconfigured/blocked; Enable→permission→subscribe→POST; Turn off→unsubscribe→DELETE) + "Install app" user-menu item when installable.

Added online/`hms:flush-drafts` re-flush to use-draft (server backup reconciliation); Background Sync (§12) registers `hms-draft-flush` → SW posts message → clients flush drafts. Authoritative mutations are NEVER queued (§10) — drafts are the only offline-writable artifact, idempotent by formKey upsert.

- Assets served: manifest JSON ✓, sw.js ✓, offline.html ✓, maskable icons ✓. Installability criteria met (manifest+icons+SW+scope+start_url).

- Push E2E: real subscription (FCM endpoint) stored with device metadata ✓; server send via VAPID → FCM 201 ✓; SW showNotification path verified (title/tag/route payload in data) ✓; **full pipeline**: operations user enabled push via UI → created real complaint (CPT-…) → notify() → web-push sent (subscription lastSeenAt updated = real FCM delivery) ✓; device replacement via endpoint upsert ✓; RBAC scoping proven (admin's device NOT pushed when admin wasn't a recipient) ✓.

Task: Splash-screen logo auto-fit — fluid responsive sizing on every device/PWA mode

Work Log:
- INSPECTION: found exactly ONE splash implementation — the loading state in src/components/hms/gate.tsx (covers initial load, hard refresh, auth/session restore, PWA launch; session.tsx renders no UI; no loading.tsx). Login-screen and header logos are panel/form branding, deliberately untouched per scope (§26).
- ROOT CAUSE of "logo too small": (1) hardcoded Tailwind h-14 w-14 (56×56 CSS px) on every viewport — zero responsiveness; (2) next/image width/height={64} with a string src and no sizes → the browser literally downloaded a 64px-wide variant; (3) source asset was logo-128.png — the whole path was dimensioned for a tiny stamp; (4) static 100vh (min-h-screen) with no safe-area or reduced-motion handling.
- FIX (src/components/hms/gate.tsx only, no duplicate splash): official /brand/logo-512.png (512×512, highest-res existing asset, ALREADY in sw.js PRECACHE_URLS → offline PWA boot renders it) rendered with width={512} priority unoptimized (no /_next/image dependency → offline-safe, SW-cached, no optimizer variance) and CSS fluid sizing w-[clamp(6rem,25vmin,15rem)] + h-auto + object-contain + max-w-full. vmin = min(vw,vh) so the limiting dimension drives sizing (width in portrait, height in landscape); 96px floor (recognizable on 320px phones) → 25vmin fluid → 240px professional cap (never dominates 4K). Keeps existing design: rounded-full ring/shadow, animate-pulse (opacity-only, cannot alter final size) + motion-reduce:animate-none, text unchanged, alt unchanged; added role="status" for SR announcement. Container: splash-viewport helper + flex centering + four max(1.25rem, env(safe-area-inset-*)) padding utilities (same pattern as shell header/mobile nav) so content can never sit under notch/cutout/system nav in standalone.
- globals.css: added .splash-viewport { min-height:100vh; min-height:100dvh; } — Tailwind's separate min-h-screen/min-h-dvh utilities emit dvh BEFORE screen (verified in built CSS → static vh would wrongly win), so the fallback needed one rule with both declarations; placed alongside existing helpers (.no-scrollbar etc.) — no new token architecture (single consumer, §22 satisfied by one clamp in one component).
- QA (scripts/splash-qa.py + splash-qa2.py, raw CDP via agent-browser; splash held visible by throttling the session request): all 19 mandated viewports (320×568 → 3840×2160, portrait+landscape+tablet+desktop+4K, realistic DPRs): logo renders square (aspect 1.000), horizontally centered (+0px deviation; stack group center −18px = logo+text block centered as designed), gap 16px, zero horizontal scroll at every size. Rendered sizes: 96px (320–430 phones P+L) → 97.5–107.5 fluid → 180–225 (desktop) → 240 cap (≥1080p/4K). Sharpness: worst case 450 device px needed vs 512 source (1440×900@2x) — all viewports OK. dvh verified (computed min-height 844px @390×844; 1080px @1920×1080). prefers-reduced-motion:reduce → computed animation none. Task ID: 27
Agent: Z.ai Code (main orchestrator)
Task: Fix PDF generation failure when company address is changed to multiple lines / two rows

Work Log:
- PIPELINE INSPECTED (no assumptions): Settings UI already uses a Textarea (modules/settings); PUT /api/v1/settings zod schema allows newlines (z.string().max(5000), key regex only constrains KEYS); Setting.value is a plain string column → multi-line saves correctly. No cache layer for settings (getBranding() reads the DB fresh per PDF; only the logo Buffer is cached) → no invalidation gap. PDF engine: ONE shared engine (src/lib/hms/pdf/engine.ts) serves all 9 registered document types via /api/v1/pdf/{type}/{id}; branding from branding.ts (contactLines); web previews use shared document-header.tsx.
- ROOT CAUSE (proven empirically + A/B at the real endpoint): pdf-lib's widthOfTextAtSize() THROWS "WinAnsi cannot encode \"\n\" (0x000a)" on any string containing a newline. drawHeader() passed each contactLines entry (the RAW address, \n intact — pdfText() deliberately preserves newlines) into fitText(), which measures unconditionally → ANY explicit line break in company_address crashed PDF generation. A/B: with a 2-line address saved, stashed fix → HTTP 500 PDF_GENERATION_FAILED; un-stashed → HTTP 200 application/pdf 94.5KB. Secondary latent hazards of the same family found and fixed: photo-grid captions (IRMS user text), QR captions, meta values, table header labels, totals label/value, banner — all measured or drawn single-line without newline flattening. Height math also under-counted multi-line entries (rule would overlap), and contactLines were capped at .slice(0,3) which would silently DROP the phone·email row for a 3-line address.
- ENGINE FIX (engine.ts): new singleLine() helper (pdfText + collapse all whitespace/newlines to one space) for single-row contexts; fitText() rewritten newline-safe (splits on \n, fits EACH physical line, rejoins — widthOfTextAtSize never sees \n); drawHeader() now FLATTENS contactLines into physical lines (flatMap split) with exact height math and NO cap — every saved address line renders and the measured container moves the green rule + bodyTop down automatically (content-driven, not a taller fixed height); right block uses singleLine'd values so left/right can never collide; defensive singleLine applied to company/title/number/meta/labels/totals/banner/photo captions/QR captions. wrap()/para()/kvGrid()/table() were already newline-safe (wrap splits on \n) — customer/supplier multi-line addresses in body kvGrids render correctly.
- BRANDING FIX (branding.ts): contactLines now = [...address.split(/\r?\n/) trimmed/filtered, phone·email] — one entry per physical line at the source; raw address still exported unchanged for other consumers.
- WEB FIX (document-header.tsx): address div gains whitespace-pre-line so previews render the exact same explicit line breaks as the generated PDF (preview == PDF); React text escaping keeps it XSS-safe; the rule is a block element below the flex header so web layout was already content-driven.
- NO DATABASE / API / cache changes needed — schema and validation already supported multi-line text; the failure was purely in the PDF rendering layer (plus web preview fidelity).
- QA (scripts/pdf-multiline-qa.ts — real API end-to-end regression, kept in repo per scripts/ convention): login → for each of 4 address shapes (1-line, 2-line, 3-line, long 4-line Brunei address) → PUT settings → GET settings (exact round-trip incl. \n) → generate ALL 9 document types (work-order, complaint, inspection-report, quotation, invoice, purchase-order, equipment-report, pm-task, payment-receipt) → assert 200 + application/pdf + %PDF magic + size; pdftotext extraction asserts EVERY address line present (quotation/invoice/work-order); special-characters test (, . - / ' ( ) & — é); original address restored + verified. RESULT: 59 passed, 0 failed. Visual: rendered T2 (2-line) + T4 (4-line) headers to PNG — logo centered, all address lines + phone·email visible, right doc block aligned, green rule BELOW all content, no overlap/clipping; header height grows with content. Browser QA: settings textarea → 3-line save → "Settings saved" toast → reload persists; quotation preview shows multi-line address (whitespace-pre-line); "Download PDF" → toast + MOHD-HMS-Quotation-QTN-2026-0004.pdf; console clean (only known localhost realtime noise); mobile 390×844 settings layout usable. dev.log: every generation logged info pdf.generated (34-89ms), zero generation_failed after fix. A/B pre-fix reproduction also captured (500 + clean structured error, no stack trace to client).
- Cleanup: user's original 2-line address restored and verified via UI + API; headless Chrome killed (OOM discipline); lint clean; tsc clean (scripts use node: APIs, no new deps).

Stage Summary:
- Multi-line company addresses are fully supported END-TO-END: textarea → API (newlines accepted) → PostgreSQL (exact storage) → PDF branding (split into physical lines) → engine (measured, content-driven header, rule below all content) → valid PDF for ALL 9 document types; web previews show identical breaks. No data loss, no truncation, no one-line limits; single-line addresses unchanged.
- Root-cause class eliminated engine-wide: no code path can pass a newline into pdf-lib widthOfTextAtSize() anymore (central singleLine/fitText contract + audit of all 20 measuring sites).
- Files: src/lib/hms/pdf/engine.ts (singleLine helper, newline-safe fitText, physical-line contact block, defensive flattening), src/lib/hms/pdf/branding.ts (address → contactLines split), src/components/hms/shared/document-header.tsx (whitespace-pre-line), scripts/pdf-multiline-qa.ts (regression harness).

Task ID: 29
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: Implement complete Forgot Password → Email verification → OTP → New Password → Confirm Password → Reset → Success → Login flow, reusing the existing auth/OTP/email/session/security architecture (no second auth system).

Work Log:
- RECON: mapped the full existing auth architecture — catch-all route → SessionProvider → Gate → login-screen (composition root) → auth/* screens driven by ?auth= URL param; API /api/v1/auth/{login,verify-email,resend-verification,forgot-password,reset-password,google,logout,session,password}; scrypt password hashing + validatePasswordStrength in lib/hms/auth.ts; DB-backed opaque sessions; in-memory sliding-window rate limiter; AuditLog via audit(); EmailOtp model + email-otp.ts (hashed 6-digit codes, purpose-scoped); SW never caches /api/*. ROOT CAUSE of the old flow: forgot-password was a legacy link-token dialog whose token had NO consuming UI — users could never actually finish a reset.
- email-otp.ts generalized to a purpose parameter (EMAIL_VERIFICATION default keeps Task 28 signatures/keys byte-compatible; new PASSWORD_RESET purpose: 60s resend cooldown, TTL 10min env-overridable via EMAIL_OTP_TTL_SEC, per-purpose dev-mailbox keys). ONE OTP system, two purposes.
- Rewrote POST /api/v1/auth/forgot-password: OTP-based request; anti-enumeration (byte-identical envelope for known/unknown emails); rate limits 5/15min per IP (env-tunable PWRESET_REQUEST_LIMIT_PER_IP) + 3/15min per IP+email; pending code reused inside cooldown (no mail-bombing); invalidates outstanding reset authorizations.
- New POST /api/v1/auth/forgot-password/verify-otp: verifies the PASSWORD_RESET OTP via the shared lib, then issues a short-lived single-use reset authorization (48 random bytes; only its SHA-256 hash stored in the existing PasswordResetToken table, 10min TTL env-overridable). One active authorization per user.
- New POST /api/v1/auth/forgot-password/resend: server-authoritative 60s cooldown (429 mirrors remaining), identical envelope for ineligible emails.
- Rewrote POST /api/v1/auth/reset-password: server-side confirmPassword check ("Passwords do not match."), EXISTING password policy, lookup by hash, ATOMIC transaction (new scrypt hash + authorization usedAt + session.deleteMany for the user + dev-mailbox cleanup), audit PASSWORD_RESET_COMPLETED; generic message for every failure shape; no auto-login.
- Frontend (all inside components/hms/auth/, same visual language): auth-flow.tsx extended with forgot / reset-otp / new-password / reset-success screens (URL-derived, state-gated, gated-context never invalidated mid-transition — root-caused a race where the stale-deep-link cleanup yanked users to welcome); login-screen "Forgot Password?" now navigates to the real flow (legacy dialog removed); new screens: forgot-screen.tsx, reset-otp-screen.tsx (6-slot InputOTP, paste/auto-advance/backspace, server-driven 60s countdown), new-password-screen.tsx (show/hide toggles, strength hint, client mirror of the existing policy; server authoritative), success-screen.tsx (Back to Login only, no auto-login); shared use-resend-countdown hook extracted and verify-screen refactored onto it (behavior identical).
- Security: OTPs hashed-only (per-user salt + purpose domain separation + server secret), single-use, 5-attempt limit, resend invalidates previous code; reset authorization hashed-only, single-use, 10min; no secrets in logs/URLs/storage; audit events REQUESTED/OTP_RESENT/OTP_VERIFIED/FAILED/COMPLETED with safe metadata only; anti-enumeration verified byte-identical; purpose separation verified (a reset code cannot verify the login-email flow).
- QA: scripts/pwreset-qa.ts (API+DB regression, 43/43) incl. negative matrix (wrong/expired/reused/exhausted OTP, forged/reused/expired authorization, mismatch, weak passwords, session revocation, old-fails/new-works, audit hygiene); scripts/pwreset-browser-qa.py (CDP, 118/118) — full UI journey per mandated viewport (320/360/375/390/412/430), wrong-code + mismatch + weak errors, show/hide toggles, back-history navigation, deep-link bypass fallbacks, offline message, reduced-motion, 0 console errors, admin-API cleanup of QA users.
- CLEANUP: all QA users/artifacts removed; dev server restarted on production-default rate limits; lint 0 errors; tsc only the pre-existing scripts/customer-onboarding-qa.ts error; dev.log clean.
- NOT changed: gate.tsx, session.tsx, shell, registry, RBAC, Google OAuth backend, login route semantics, customer onboarding, PWA runtime/SW, DB schema (PasswordResetToken + EmailOtp reused; no migration needed).

Stage Summary:
- Complete real password recovery now ships: Login → Forgot Password (dedicated screen) → eligibility check + 6-digit OTP email → Verify Your Email (6-slot OTP, 60s server-driven resend cooldown) → Create New Password + Confirm → atomic reset (hash + all sessions revoked + audit) → Success → Back to Login → old password fails, new password works. Google/email login, customer onboarding, RBAC and PWA behavior unchanged.
- The legacy broken link-token dialog is fully superseded; PasswordResetToken now stores only SHA-256 hashes of one-time authorizations.
- Honest limitations: this sandbox has no external SMTP provider — email delivery uses the established MOHD.HMS EMAIL channel pattern (structured queued log + dev-mailbox Setting diagnostic); production wiring point is identical to the Task 28 verification emails (no code change needed when a provider is configured). PASSWORD_RESET_FAILED audits carry only {email?, reason}. Install-prompt card can still overlay on small landscape (pre-existing Task 25 note).

Task ID: 31
Agent: Z.ai Code (main orchestrator)
Task: FIX PHOTO / VIDEO / PDF / DOCUMENT UPLOAD AND PROCESSING USING THE EXISTING LOCAL S3-COMPATIBLE (MINIO API) OBJECT STORAGE — root-cause fix of "One of the images could not be processed" + "0 photos"; then push to GitHub.

Work Log:
- SURVEY: traced the complete real pipeline (browser XHR → POST /api/v1/irms/reports/[id]/photos → assertImageUpload → savePhotoVariants (sharp) → local filesystem uploads/irms → Prisma InspectionPhoto → photoItemDto → file routes). Found the codebase had NO MinIO/S3 anywhere — storage was local filesystem despite the spec mandating S3/MinIO as authoritative storage.
- ROOT CAUSES (verified, not guessed):
  1. assertImageUpload trusted the CLIENT-declared MIME (file.type) + extension. Mobile cameras/pickers frequently report imperfect values (image/jpg, empty MIME, HEIC) → valid photos rejected or misrouted.
  2. The route's per-file catch swallowed the REAL exception (sharp decoder error, storage error) and returned the generic "One of the images could not be processed" — root cause invisible (§3 forbids hiding exceptions).
  3. Failure path was non-atomic: rows created for earlier files in a batch persisted even when a later file failed (partial state); the frontend then showed misleading "0 photos" (server count) while files were queued/failed.
  4. No HEIC/HEIF strategy — iPhones produce HEIC by default; sharp cannot decode it; users got a dead-end generic error.
- OBJECT STORAGE: MinIO no longer distributes public binaries (dl.min.io 410, GitHub releases have no assets) and Docker is unavailable. Implemented the mandated architecture with an S3-COMPATIBLE local object store (s3rver under bun) speaking the EXACT MinIO S3 API, run as mini-service `mini-services/object-storage` (port 3090, bucket hms-files, creds S3RVER/S3RVER, data in gitignored .storage/s3). App code uses the official `minio` v8 client pointed via S3_* env — pointing production at a real MinIO server is a pure env change.
- NEW centralized StorageService `src/lib/hms/storage/index.ts` (§49): put/get/stat/remove/removePrefix/healthCheck + assertSafeKey (path-traversal guard); single client, credentials server-side only; bucket stays fully private (all downloads via authenticated RBAC routes — no public policy, no CORS, no presigned leakage).
- REWROTE `src/lib/hms/irms/storage.ts` (same exported API surface, S3-backed):
  - sniffImageType(): magic-byte detection (JPEG/PNG/WebP) — bytes decide, not client MIME (§4); HEIC/HEIF/AVIF detected via ftyp brand with actionable "set camera to JPEG" message (§7).
  - preparePhotoUpload(): decode + EXIF-rotate + derive display(1600px q82)/thumb(320px q78) variants fully IN MEMORY before anything is stored (§5); original bytes stored untouched (§48).
  - storePreparedPhoto(): store objects → confirm → then DB row completed; any failure removes uploaded objects + rolls back rows (§16, no orphans either direction).
  - UploadValidationError taxonomy with stable codes: INVALID_FILE / UNSUPPORTED_FORMAT / FILE_TOO_LARGE / IMAGE_PROCESSING_FAILED / STORAGE_UPLOAD_FAILED, each with an honest user message (§19).
  - readVariantFile/deleteFiles/deleteReportDir now S3-backed (keys irms/{reportId}/...); signatures stored as irms/{reportId}/signatures/{id}.png.
- REWROTE photos POST route: two-phase (validate+decode ALL → persist ALL), per-failure rollback, real exception logging with structured `image.processing.failed` entries (now visible in dev.log), ApiError(422, <code>, honest message). Same treatment for the signatures route.
- HEALTH: /api/health/ready now also probes object storage (put+remove probe) → {database, storage} (§25/§26).
- FRONTEND `irms-photo-manager.tsx`: fail-fast client validation (unsupported type / HEIC / >15MB "Image is too large. Maximum allowed size is 15 MB." / empty), empty-MIME rescue via extension, XHR timeout 180s + "Upload failed. Please check your connection and try again.", progress cap 99% shows "Processing…" then ✓, honest count line "{n} photos stored · queue: X uploaded, Y failed, Z in progress" (§21 — no more misleading bare "0 photos"), Retry re-queues the SAME file (idempotent — server-side unique keys per upload).
- MIGRATION `scripts/migrate-uploads-to-s3.ts`: copied all 11 existing photos (33 objects) + 2 signatures into S3 with `irms/` prefixed keys and rewrote DB references; idempotent; legacy uploads/ dir then removed (no local permanent storage remains).
- QA `scripts/upload-pipeline-qa.ts` (real API + real S3 + real DB): 70+ checks — JPG/PNG/WebP/large(3.2MB)/wrong-declared-MIME/numeric+long filenames upload ✓; original byte-identical in S3 (sha256); EXIF-rotated display baked to 640x480; HEIC/corrupt/text/empty/oversized rejected with exact codes; failures create ZERO rows/objects; retry idempotent; duplicate uploads → unique keys; customer blocked from staff report (upload/read/list); anon file access 401; photo delete + report delete remove ALL S3 objects (no orphans); signature roundtrip ✓. → ALL CHECKS PASSED.
- BROWSER QA (agent-browser): logged in as supervisor, DRAFT report → PHOTOS tab: uploaded camera-photo.jpg + site-plan.png → 201/201, auto-numbered B001/B002, thumbs render (naturalWidth>0), queue shows live progress; invalid notes.txt → inline "Unsupported file type…" + Retry; select→Delete→confirm → "1 photo deleted", renumbered B002→B001; mobile 375px: no horizontal overflow, honest "queue: 0 uploaded, 2 in progress" captured live; console errors: none. Screenshots reviewed (desktop + mobile).
- FINAL CONSISTENCY: 74/74 DB-referenced objects exist in S3, 0 missing, 0 legacy paths; stale QA artifacts removed; lint clean.

Stage Summary:
- Uploads now WORK end-to-end (camera/gallery/JPG/PNG/WebP/large/EXIF-rotated/wrong-MIME/numeric filenames) and every failure ends in a specific, actionable error — the generic "One of the images could not be processed" is gone because the causes are fixed, not the message.
- Architecture: S3-compatible (MinIO API) object storage = authoritative file store via ONE centralized StorageService; DB = metadata/keys; Redis untouched; filesystem no longer used for any permanent storage. Production MinIO = S3_* env swap.
- Videos/PDFs/documents: no module currently persists these (PDFs generate on-demand and stream; no /uploads writes anywhere else) — the centralized service is ready for them when needed (spec §30–§34 satisfied by routing through storage service; nothing stored outside S3).
- Reports: final report content mirrors worklog §Task 31 (root cause, layers, changes, QA, verifications, remaining: none; production MinIO endpoint to be provided by ops via S3_* env).

- TYPECHECK BASELINE: bunx tsc --noEmit found 46 errors in email-scope files (27× resolve.ts, 9× service.ts, 3× automations/route.ts, 2× render.ts, 1× attachments.ts, 1× each in automations/[id], templates, config/test-send, templates/[id]/test-send, logs, meta routes) + pre-existing unrelated errors (.next/types/validator.ts, scripts/customer-onboarding-qa.ts, irms photos route ×2, src/lib/hms/email-otp.ts — all OUTSIDE the defined scope, left untouched and reported).
- FIXED src/lib/hms/email/resolve.ts (27 errors, all root-caused to wrong Prisma field/relation names — each bad select/include silently dropped the whole select, so the result types lost every relation): Complaint has NO location → COMPLAINT_LOCATION now resolves Complaint→equipment→location.name (L70-81); WorkOrder assignee relation is `technician` not `assignedTechnician` (L94/105); nested complaint select likewise via equipment.location (L93/103); Quotation date field is `quotationDate` not `issueDate` (L118); Invoice date field is `invoiceDate` not `issueDate` (L135); PmTask assignee is `technician` (L166/174); Equipment has no `code` → uses `assetTag` (L165/173); InspectionReport.inspector is a TechnicianProfile → name via inspector.user.name (L198/205); InventoryItem stock fields are `stockQty`/`minStockQty` not `quantity`/`minQuantity` (L246-247).
- FIXED src/lib/hms/email/service.ts: ResolutionContext imported from ./resolve (it lives there, not in types.ts, L23/26-28); removed impossible `include: { template: true }` on EmailAutomation (schema has NO template relation — only templateKey) and runOneAutomation now fetches the template by canonical key itself (L215-217, L257-259); runOneAutomation declares its own local `skipped` array and returns it so runEmailAutomations still aggregates skip reasons (L238, L301-302, L330, L334, L222-225); assigneeEmailFor WorkOrder/PmTask use the real `technician` relation (L457-463); renderTemplate is now async → awaited all 5 call sites (L90, L165, L287, L662, L668).
- FIXED sendTestEmail (service.ts L655-660) — CONTRACT FIX #1: the admin UI test-send selector sends the template KEY (meta.templates[].key) in the templateId field while the backend only looked up by DB id → now looks up by id FIRST then by key (either accepted); templateKey/templateVersion written to the EmailLog stay the found template's real values.
- FIXED src/lib/hms/email/render.ts + layout.ts: renderTemplate is now `async` (wrapBrandedEmail awaits getBranding + a settings lookup and already returned Promise<string> — the old sync signature was unsound, html field was a Promise); wrapBrandedEmail now accepts `logoSrc` in opts (previously declared-but-ignored by callers, hardcoded cid) with default cid:mohd-hms-logo.
- FIXED src/lib/hms/email/attachments.ts L90: PDF engine returns Uint8Array → ResolvedAttachment.buffer needs Buffer → Buffer.from(built.bytes).
- FIXED src/lib/hms/email/workflows.ts: exported EMAIL_AUTOMATION_EVENTS (automations + meta routes import it).
- FIXED src/app/api/v1/email/automations/route.ts: GET no longer includes nonexistent `template` relation — fetches the referenced templates by key in one query and attaches `template` per row (the UI binds a.template.name); POST returns ok(created, 201) (ok's 2nd arg is a number, not {status}).
- FIXED src/app/api/v1/email/automations/[id]/route.ts: automationOf() does the same manual template join so GET/PATCH/DELETE still return a `template` object.
- FIXED src/app/api/v1/email/templates/route.ts L81: ok(..., 201) signature.
- FIXED Errors.toMany → Errors.tooMany in config/test-send/route.ts L23 and templates/[id]/test-send/route.ts L22 (ApiError factory is named tooMany).
- FIXED src/app/api/v1/email/logs/route.ts L56: removed invalid `hasBody: false` from the Prisma select (hasBody is not a column; the okList map already appends the honest hasBody:false for every list row).
- CONTRACT FIX #2 (email-tab.tsx saveConfig L201-213): the config PATCH payload sent NULLS for empty fields (smtpHost/smtpUser/fromName/fromEmail/replyTo/testRecipient) but the PATCH zod schema only accepts strings (only smtpPassword is nullable) — every save with any empty field would 400; now sends "" for cleared strings and OMITS smtpPort/timeoutMs when empty ("" never wipes a stored password — that path is unchanged).
- CONTRACT FIX #3 (email-automations.tsx): POST/PATCH payloads sent senderName/senderEmail/replyTo as null → zod rejects; now "" (backend treats "" as "use default"). Attachments were sent as an array of KIND STRINGS while AUTOMATION_INPUT expects [{kind}] objects → now .map(kind => ({kind})) in both duplicate (L223) and editor save (L319).
- CONTRACT FIX #4 (email-automations.tsx HistoryRow L61-69, L747/758/760): history rows bound to `to`/`attempts`/`error` but the API returns `toEmail`/`attemptCount`/`lastError` → frontend bindings fixed to the backend's real output.
- CONTRACT FIX #5 (email-logs.tsx L24-56, L242/281/284/289/419/431/439): list rows bound to `to` → toEmail; detail bound to senderName/senderEmail/automationName → the API's fromName/fromEmail/automation{name}; attachmentRefs typed as JSON string but the detail API returns the PARSED array → parseAttachments now consumes the array directly; TEST badges keyed on nonexistent category="TEST" → now use the real isTest flag (list + detail).
- CONTRACT FIX #6 (email-templates.tsx L168/186/202/260/518): create-form category defaulted to "GENERAL" which is NOT in EMAIL_CATEGORIES (zod enum) → every template created without touching the category select would 400; defaults are now "SYSTEM".
- VERIFIED OK (no change needed): config GET returns every Overview binding incl. hasPassword (EmailConfigSafe); health endpoint shape matches the Overview cards (sentToday/failedToday/queued/retrying/lastSuccess{at,to,subject}/lastFailure{at,to,error}/smtp{configured,host,port,security,lastVerifyAt,lastVerifyOk}); meta route shape matches the UI vocabularies; automations toggle PATCH sends {enabled} only; templates list/preview/versions/test-send/duplicate bindings all match; settings/index.tsx wiring untouched (no errors).

- SCHEMA (+EmailConfig singleton with AES-256-GCM encrypted SMTP secret, +EmailTemplate/+EmailTemplateVersion immutable snapshots, +EmailAutomation with controlled recipient-rule/condition/attachment JSON, +EmailLog authoritative job+history with unique [eventId, automationId] idempotency key); db:push OK.
- LIB src/lib/hms/email/: crypto (AES-256-GCM v1 envelope), config (safe DTO — password NEVER returned; masked update semantics), provider (nodemailer SMTP + error classification TEMPORARY/PERMANENT/AUTH/RECIPIENT/NETWORK/PROVIDER + REAL verify), layout (allowlist HTML sanitizer + branded table shell with cid logo + centralized footer from company settings), catalog (22 canonical templates for events that actually exist incl. 2 OTP + letters + IRMS + PM + purchases + system), resolve (server-side variable resolution from authorized data), render (escaped substitution, unknown vars dropped, §11 validation), attachments (entity-authorized resolution via central PDF engine/MinIO, never raw keys), service (queueDirect/sendOtpEmail/queueLetterEmail/runEmailAutomations with idempotency+dedupe+rate-limit, tickEmailWorker claim-guard + backoff 1m/5m/15m + DEAD_LETTER + body retention 30d, testConnection/sendTestEmail honest, retry/cancel, preview sample data, emailHealth real metrics), bootstrap (idempotent templates+automations seeding), workflows (EMAIL_AUTOMATION handler registered on 20 real event types).
- INTEGRATION: auth OTP → sendOtpEmail (critical, bypasses channel gate; dev-mailbox contract preserved); EMAIL_SEND workflow → queueDirect GENERAL_NOTIFICATION (+ in-app row kept); notify() EMAIL channel → EmailService; letters send → queueLetterEmail with LETTER_PDF from MinIO, letters approve → LETTER_APPROVED event; scheduler: email worker tick every 10s + new INVOICE_DUE_SOON (≤3d) and QUOTATION_EXPIRING (≤7d) scans (same scheduler — no second one); automation setting email_max_per_automation_hour added.

Audits: EMAIL_CONFIG_UPDATED, EMAIL_TEMPLATE_*, EMAIL_AUTOMATION_*, EMAIL_TEST_SENT, EMAIL_RETRIED, EMAIL_CANCELED — never any secret content.

Fixes: automation-tab copy + email_max_per_automation_hour field; Settings TabsList flex-wrap (390px overflow root cause).
